This approach offers three key advantages: (1) no training data or manual annotations, (2) object-agnostic generalization to arbitrary object types, and (3) significantly reduced development time.

</details>


### [35] [UniVerse: A Unified Modulation Framework for Segmentation-Free,Disentangled Multi-Concept Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00351)
*Quynh Phung,Sandesh Ghimire,Minsi Hu,Chung-Chi Tsai,Jia-Bin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需分割掩码的统一调制框架UniVerse，用于在扩散变换器中实现解耦的多概念个性化，能够在复杂场景中精确提取和操控多个视觉概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理包含多个物体的图像时难以准确定位和提取特定概念，且依赖分割监督或泛化能力差，限制了对个体概念的有效解耦与操作。

Method: 提出UniVerse框架，通过学习将复杂场景分解为特定概念表示，并以统一方式重组，实现在无显式分割掩码情况下的可组合与可分解概念提取。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，UniVerse在定位精度和视觉保真度上显著优于现有最先进方法，能在杂乱场景中精确提取目标概念。

Conclusion: UniVerse实现了无需分割监督的多概念解耦个性化，提升了个性化视觉理解与生成的灵活性、可解释性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Personalized visual understanding has advanced significantly, yet existing approaches struggle to localize and extract specific concepts when input images contain multiple objects. Many prior methods rely heavily on segmentation-based supervision or exhibit poor compositional generalization, limiting their ability to accurately disentangle and manipulate individual concepts. In this work, we propose UniVerse, a Unified Modulation Framework for segmentation-free, disentangled multi-concept personalization in diffusion transformers. Our method allows for composable and decomposable concept extraction, enabling fine-grained localization and representation of target objects without explicit segmentation masks. UniVerse learns to decompose complex scenes into concept-specific representations and then compose them in a unified manner, enabling robust personalization across diverse visual contexts. Through extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, we demonstrate that UniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both localization accuracy and visual fidelity. Qualitative and quantitative results show that our approach can precisely extract target concepts in cluttered scenes, paving the way for more flexible, interpretable, and personalized visual generation and understanding.

</details>


### [36] [HiGS: A Hierarchical Rendering Architecture for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00352)
*Dawid Pająk,Martin Bisson,Rodolfo Lima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hierarchically Tiled Gaussian Splatting (HiGS)，通过为分区和光栅化分别使用粗粒度宏块和细粒度渲染块，解决了3D高斯点阵中性能瓶颈问题，显著提升了渲染速度，最高达到原始3DGS的15.8倍，同时保持精确的前后alpha合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯点阵(3DGS)在实时新视角合成中广泛应用，但其管道将空间分区和光栅化绑定到单一瓦片大小，导致密集区域成为帧时间的主要瓶颈。需要一种方法来解耦这两个过程以提高效率。

Method: 提出了一种分层瓦片化的高斯点阵方法(HiGS)，其中空间分区在粗粒度的宏块上进行，而光栅化则在这些宏块内的细粒度渲染瓦片上执行。这样可以按每个宏块中的高斯分布比例分配光栅化工作，避免了因少数密集瓦片造成的串行处理问题。

Result: 在测试场景中，HiGS相比原始3DGS渲染速度快至15.8倍，并且优于所有评估的其他光栅化器，同时保持了准确的前向-后向alpha合成效果。

Conclusion: HiGS通过分离空间分区与光栅化的尺度，有效解决了3DGS中存在的性能瓶颈问题，实现了显著的速度提升，是实现实时高质量渲染的一个重要进展。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the standard for real-time novel view synthesis on commodity GPUs. Its pipeline ties spatial partitioning and rasterization to one tile size, yet the two pull in opposite directions: partitioning, which bins and depth-sorts gaussians, grows cheaper with larger tiles, while rasterization gets cheaper with smaller ones. Prior acceleration work reduces the cost of individual stages but keeps both locked to that single scale, where a few dense tiles dominate frame time. We present Hierarchically Tiled Gaussian Splatting (HiGS), which gives each its own scale: partitioning runs over coarse macro-tiles, while rasterization runs over the fine render tiles within them. Rasterization work is then issued in proportion to the gaussians in each macro-tile rather than per tile, so dense regions spread across many parallel units instead of serializing through one. Across tested scenes, HiGS renders up to 15.8x faster than the original 3DGS and outperforms every other rasterizer we evaluate, while preserving exact front-to-back alpha compositing.

</details>


### [37] [LFA: Layer Feature Attention for Run-Time Introspection of 2D Object Detectors in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00372)
*Mert Keser,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Layer Feature Attention (LFA) 的轻量级内省方法，通过注意力机制聚合骨干网络多个层次的特征来预测目标检测器的失败，从而提升自动驾驶中的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检测器错误预测方法仅使用最后一层特征或手工设计的统计量，忽略了来自早期层的有价值信息，而这些信息在不同视觉抽象层级上对检测错误具有不同的表现力。

Method: 提出LFA方法，利用注意力机制从骨干网络的多个层次中学习聚合特征，并端到端地学习各层的重要性权重，以提高错误预测性能并增强可解释性。

Result: 在KITTI和BDD100K数据集上的实验表明，LFA在多种检测器架构下均优于单层基线方法，达到了当前最优的内省性能。

Conclusion: LFA能有效利用多层特征进行检测失败预测，不仅提升了可靠性，还提供了关于哪些特征层次对失败最敏感的可解释分析，有助于安全的自动驾驶系统部署。

Abstract: Reliable object detection is critical for automated driving, yet even state-of-the-art detectors inevitably make errors that can compromise safety. Introspection methods that predict detector failures enable safer deployment by triggering fallback mechanisms or alerting human operators. However, existing approaches rely solely on last-layer features or hand-crafted statistics, discarding valuable information from earlier layers that capture different levels of visual abstraction. We propose Layer Feature Attention (LFA), a lightweight introspection method that learns to aggregate features from multiple backbone layers through an attention mechanism. Our key insight is that detection errors manifest differently across feature hierarchies: low-level layers capture fine-grained details essential for detecting small or occluded objects, while high-level layers encode semantic information for scene understanding. LFA learns layer importance weights end-to-end, enabling both improved error prediction and interpretable analysis of which feature levels are most indicative of detector failures. Extensive experiments on KITTI and BDD100K demonstrate that LFA achieves state-of-the-art introspection performance, outperforming single-layer baselines across multiple detector architectures.

</details>


### [38] [Score-Control for Hallucination Reduction in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00377)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Abdul Wasi,Chau Pham,Vishnu Suresh Lokhande,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方差引导的分数调制（VSM）策略，通过控制分数雅可比矩阵来减少扩散模型中的幻觉现象，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低了约25%的幻觉发生率，并提供了新的基准数据集用于系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像生成中存在幻觉问题，即生成不真实或不符合数据分布的样本，影响了其可靠性和可信度。本文旨在从理论上解释并解决这一问题。

Method: 通过建立幻觉概率与学习到的分数函数Lipschitz常数之间的联系，提出VSM方法调节分数函数的雅可比矩阵，降低分数平滑性，从而更接近真实的分数函数以减少幻觉。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，VSM能有效减少幻觉（最多约25%），同时保持高保真度和多样性；并提出了两个具有极端语义变化的基准数据集用于幻觉评估。

Conclusion: VSM为提升扩散模型生成结果的可靠性提供了一个有原则的解决方案，有助于推动更可信的生成式AI发展。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the backbone of modern generative AI, powering advances in vision, language, audio and other modalities. Despite their success, they suffer from hallucinations, implausible samples that lie outside the support of true data distribution, which degrade reliability and trust. In this work, we first empirically confirm previously proposed hypothesis that score smoothness causes hallucinations in Image Generation diffusion models and provide a density-based perspective. We further formalize this notion by linking the hallucinations probability mass to lipschitz constant of the learned score function. Motivated by this, we introduce a Variance-Guided Score Modulation (VSM) strategy that controls the score Jacobian, in turn reducing score smoothness and better approximating the ground truth score that decreases hallucinations. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach reduces hallucinations (up to ~25%) while maintaining high fidelity and diversity, providing a principled step toward more reliable diffusion-based image generation. We also propose two benchmark datasets with extreme semantic variation for systematic hallucination evaluation. Code and Datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/bhosalems/VSM.

</details>


### [39] [Non-Learning Low-Light Stereo Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00379)
*Jason Wang,Lucas Nguyen,Hyunseung Eom,Wei Xu,Qi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于从严重噪声图像中进行视差估计的非学习立体框架，利用Junction场（FoJ）在强噪声下保留稳定的粗略视觉特征，并结合边界感知的半全局匹配方法生成更精确的稀疏视差图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严重噪声影响下，传统立体匹配方法难以有效区分真实纹理与噪声，导致视差估计性能下降，因此需要一种对噪声鲁棒的结构化特征表示方法。

Method: 采用Junction场（FoJ）提取在强噪声下仍稳定的粗略结构特征，构建代价体，并结合动态调整平滑惩罚的边界感知半全局匹配（SGM）方法，以保持真实的视差不连续性。

Result: 在常用基准数据集上，该方法在未遮挡像素上的稀疏视差图精度优于近期的立体匹配算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的非学习框架通过利用结构化特征和自适应平滑策略，在严重噪声条件下实现了更鲁棒和准确的视差估计。

Abstract: We present a non-learning stereo framework for disparity estimation from severely noisy images. Using the Field of Junctions (FoJ), it retains coarse visual features stable under severe noise for cost volume construction while discarding fine textures inseparable from photon noise. The resulting structural information guides boundary-aware Semi-Global Matching (SGM) that dynamically adapts smoothness penalties to preserve true disparity discontinuities. The output is a sparse disparity map more accurate than those of recent stereo algorithms over unmasked pixels on widely-used benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [40] [SUPREME: A Multi-GPU Framework for Reproducible Image Unlearning Method Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00380)
*Petros Andreou,Jamie Lanyon,Axel Finke,Georgina Cosma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUPREME是一个开源的多GPU分布式框架，用于高效评估图像分类中的机器遗忘方法，支持多种模型、指标和场景，并在Pins Face Recognition数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘评估方法通常在单个GPU上运行，限制了多随机种子实验的效率，导致计算成本高。为了加速评估过程，需要一个能够利用多GPU资源的分布式框架。

Method: 提出SUPREME框架，采用注册表设计以灵活集成新方法、模型和评估指标，并构建支持多加速器和多种精度模式的多GPU架构，实现训练、遗忘和评估流程的并行化。

Result: 在ResNet18和ViT模型上，针对全类和随机样本遗忘场景，使用十个不同随机种子进行实验，结果表明SUPREME能有效提升评估效率，并验证了其在真实人脸识别数据集上的适用性。

Conclusion: SUPREME为机器遗忘研究提供了一个可扩展、高效的分布式评估平台，推动了该领域的大规模实验与比较研究。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining it from scratch. Evaluating an unlearning method requires repeating training, unlearning, and evaluation across multiple seeds, which is computationally expensive. To our knowledge, existing image classification unlearning frameworks run on a single GPU, which limits how many seeds can be evaluated in reasonable time. We introduce SUPREME, an open-source framework that distributes these stages across multiple GPUs. SUPREME makes three contributions: a registry-based design for adding new methods, metrics, models, and scenarios; a multi-GPU architecture supporting multiple accelerators and precision modes; and a demonstration on Pins Face Recognition using ResNet18 and ViT under full-class and random-sample unlearning across ten seeds. The framework is available at https://github.com/pedroandreou/supreme-unlearning.

</details>


### [41] [αDepth: Learning Single-Pass Soft Boundary Decomposition for Stereo Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00386)
*Xiang Zhang,Yang Zhang,Lukas Mehl,Karlis Martins Briedis,Markus Gross,Christopher Schroers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为αDepth的分层表示方法，用于解决软边界（如头发和散焦模糊）在立体转换中的建模难题。通过分层颜色和深度估计以及新设计的循环Alpha表示（CAR），实现了无需人工干预的高保真立体转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于前景与背景在软边界处存在模糊混合，准确建模软边界（如头发和散焦模糊）是立体转换中的一大挑战。现有深度模型多预测单层深度，在软边界处易产生深度对应歧义；而传统抠图技术虽可捕捉不透明度，但在复杂多目标场景下表现不佳且常需人工干预。

Method: 提出αDepth，一种分解软边界的分层表示方法：首先估计软边界处的分层颜色和深度以解决颜色与深度的混合歧义；针对复杂多目标场景，设计了循环Alpha表示（CAR），将范式从全局目标提取转变为局部边界分解，支持无需手动引导的高效场景级推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，αDepth在立体转换任务中达到最先进水平，能有效消除软边界处的背景渗色和结构扭曲问题。

Conclusion: αDepth通过引入分层表示和CAR机制，成功解决了软边界建模中的混合歧义与多目标复杂性问题，实现了高质量、无需人工干预的立体转换，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurately modeling soft boundaries, e.g., hair and defocus blur, is a fundamental challenge in stereo conversion due to the ambiguous blending of foreground and background. Existing depth models primarily predict single-layer depth, leading to ambiguity in depth correspondence at soft boundaries. While matting techniques can capture opacity for layered modeling, they often struggle in complex scenes with multiple targets and usually require user intervention. This paper introduces αDepth, a layered representation that decomposes soft boundaries for high-fidelity stereo conversion. Specifically, we first resolve mixed color and depth ambiguity by estimating layered color and depth values at soft boundaries. Considering complex multi-target scenes, we design a Circular Alpha Representation (CAR) that shifts the paradigm from global target extraction to local boundary decomposition. Unlike prior matting methods restricted to a single foreground/background, CAR enables efficient scene-level inference without manual guidance. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that αDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance in stereo conversion, eliminating background bleeding and structural distortions at soft boundaries.

</details>


### [42] [Zamba2-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00390)
*Hassan Shapourian,Kasra Hejazi,Olabode M. Sule,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zamba2-VL 是基于 Zamba2 架构的视觉-语言模型系列，结合 Mamba2 状态空间层与少量共享 Transformer 块，在多项视觉任务中表现优于现有 SSM 和混合 VLM 模型，并在推理延迟上显著优于同类 Transformer 模型，尤其适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发高效、低延迟的视觉-语言模型，克服传统 Transformer 在长序列处理中的高计算成本问题，推动适用于边缘和终端设备的轻量级多模态模型发展。

Method: 基于 Zamba2 混合架构（Mamba2 状态空间模型 + 少量 Transformer 块），构建视觉-语言模型 Zamba2-VL，并在多种基准任务上评估其性能与推理效率。

Result: Zamba2-VL 在图像理解、推理、OCR、定位和计数等任务上与主流开源 Transformer VLM 性能相当，且在相同参数规模下 TTFT 降低约一个数量级，尤其在 1.2B 和 2.7B 小模型上优势更明显。

Conclusion: Zamba2-VL 成功结合了 SSM 的高效性与 Transformer 的表达能力，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟，是面向边缘部署的高效多模态模型的有力候选。

Abstract: We present Zamba2-VL, a suite of vision-language models built on Zamba2, a hybrid language-model architecture combining Mamba2 state-space layers with a small number of shared transformer blocks. Across a broad range of image understanding, reasoning, OCR, grounding, and counting benchmarks, Zamba2-VL is competitive with leading Transformer-based open-weight VLMs of comparable scale, including the Molmo2, Qwen3-VL, and InternVL3.5 families, and substantially outperforms prior SSM-based and hybrid VLMs such as VL-Mamba, Cobra, and mmMamba. Inheriting the near-linear prefill compute and small, near-constant recurrent state of its Zamba2 backbone, Zamba2-VL delivers roughly an order of magnitude lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) than these Transformer baselines at matched parameter scale, with the efficiency gap most pronounced at the smaller 1.2B and 2.7B scales most relevant to on-device and edge deployment. We release three models -- 1.2B, 2.7B, and 7B -- together with inference code at https://huggingface.co/collections/Zyphra/zamba2-vl.

</details>


### [43] [Rethinking Amortized Neural Representations for High-Resolution Terrain Elevation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00404)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于地形高程数据的隐式神经表示新方法 HUVR+SIREN，通过改进现有超网络架构，在保持低存储开销的同时显著提升了高度和导数的重建精度，并验证了其在大规模地形数据上的有效性与紧凑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐式神经表示方法多针对自然图像设计，直接应用于地形高程数据时存在性能差距，且单独为每个瓦片训练INR不可扩展，因此需要一种高效、可扩展并适用于地形数据的共享网络表示方法。

Method: 构建了一个1米/像素分辨率的地形数据基准测试平台，统一评估三种代表性摊销神经表示方法；在此基础上提出HUVR+SIREN，将最强基准方法HUVR的坐标解码器替换为平滑且解析可微的SIREN解码器，以更好适应地形高度场特性。

Result: HUVR+SIREN在高度和导数保真度上均取得最佳表现，无需额外每瓦片存储，解码成本更低，并能耐受极端量化压缩而几乎无质量损失，形成紧凑的地形神经格式；消融实验表明当前每瓦片瓶颈已接近极限，剩余性能差距主要源于共享超网络的架构设计。

Conclusion: 针对地形高程数据的隐式神经表示需专门设计，HUVR+SIREN通过适配解码器结构显著提升性能，为大规模地形数据提供了一种高效、精确且紧凑的神经表示方案。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) model a signal as a continuous coordinate-to-value function. For terrain elevation data, this supports analytic derivatives, arbitrary-resolution decoding, and a smooth surface model of the underlying heightfield. However, fitting and storing a separate INR for every tile does not scale to large terrain datasets. Amortized neural representations reduce this cost with a shared network: a new tile is mapped to a compact per-tile payload, and a shared decoder reconstructs the heightfield from it. Most such methods are hypernetworks that predict the payload in a single forward pass, while others recover it through a short per-tile optimization. These methods were developed primarily for natural images, and their suitability for terrain heightfields remains unclear. We introduce a controlled benchmark on a 1 m/pixel terrain dataset and evaluate three representative methods under a unified protocol. Observing a clear cross-domain gap, we propose HUVR+SIREN, a hypernetwork that adapts the strongest benchmarked method (HUVR) by replacing its coordinate decoder with a smooth, analytically differentiable one. It attains the best height and derivative fidelity on the benchmark with no additional per-tile storage and lower decode cost, and tolerates aggressive post-training quantization with negligible quality loss, giving a compact terrain neural format. Ablations and diagnostics further identify which design choices transfer to terrain and show that the per-tile bottleneck is already near its useful limit, leaving the remaining gap in the shared hypernetwork's architectural design.

</details>


### [44] [4D Radar Meets LiDAR and Camera: Cooperative Perception under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00416)
*Melih Yazgan,Iramm Hamdard,Qiyuan Wu,J. Marius Zoellner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于4D成像雷达的协同感知方法，通过引入多智能体融合的多普勒引导空间注意力机制，提升了自动驾驶在恶劣天气下的感知鲁棒性，并发布了相应的数据集和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对摄像头和LiDAR在恶劣天气下性能下降导致协同感知脆弱的问题，探索更鲁棒的感知模态。

Method: 将4D成像雷达作为抗天气干扰的模态引入协同感知，设计了多普勒引导的空间注意力机制用于多智能体信息融合，并扩展了两种代表性骨干网络：雷达-相机流程和LiDAR-雷达互补流程。

Result: 在OPV2V-R和Adver-City-R等增强基准上实验表明，该方法在雾天和雨天表现出强健的鲁棒性，尤其在雷达替代退化的LiDAR时有显著提升；在MAN TruckScenes上的验证显示其可超越仿真实现实际迁移。

Conclusion: 4D成像雷达是一种适用于全天候协同感知的鲁棒感知模态，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Cooperative perception is important for autonomous driving but remains fragile when cameras and LiDAR degrade in adverse weather. We address this challenge by integrating 4D imaging radar as a weather-robust modality into collaborative perception and introducing a Doppler-guided spatial attention mechanism for multi-agent fusion. Our approach extends two representative backbones: a radar-camera pipeline where radar substitutes LiDAR, and a LiDAR-radar pipeline where radar complements LiDAR. To support evaluation, we release radar-augmented benchmarks, OPV2V-R and Adver-City-R, with physics-based LiDAR degradation. Experiments show strong robustness gains in fog and rain, including substantial improvements when radar replaces degraded LiDAR. Additional validation on MAN TruckScenes demonstrates transfer beyond simulation. Overall, our results highlight 4D imaging radar as a robust modality for all-weather collaborative perception. Dataset and code are available at: https://url.fzi.de/SlimComm.

</details>


### [45] [Detect Before You Leap: Mirage Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00435)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Md. Shaown Miah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TC-LIA的模型无关方法，用于在视觉语言模型（VLM）生成回答前检测其是否缺乏必要的视觉证据（即“mirage”现象），通过分析CLIP视觉编码器中各层的图像块-文本对齐轨迹，并结合多种特征实现高精度的三类检测，显著降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在缺少或无关视觉证据时仍可能生成看似合理的错误答案（称为“mirage”现象），这在医疗和文档问答等关键领域尤为危险，因此需要在模型输出前检测并防止此类错误。

Method: 提出Text-Conditioned Layer-wise Internal Alignment (TC-LIA) 方法，通过将CLIP ViT-H/14编码器各层的图像块令牌投影到最终的CLIP嵌入空间，计算其与问题嵌入的相似性，追踪视觉信息在不同网络层中的演化；结合最终图像-文本余弦相似度、后期top-k块对齐、前后层增益、层间斜率等特征，并融合像素统计、零样本域路由和VLM自评估构建集成系统。

Result: 在五个VQA领域、三种输入条件和十二个VLM骨干网络上的实验表明，最佳系统实现了约94.6%-94.7%的三类检测准确率，且幻觉率低于3%，而基线模型的幻觉率在21.7%至66.6%之间。

Conclusion: TC-LIA能够有效识别VLM在无视觉支持情况下的潜在错误响应，显著提升其可靠性和安全性，适用于多种模型和应用场景，为预发布阶段的VLM风险控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can produce confident visual answers even when the required visual evidence is missing, blank, or unrelated to the question. This failure mode, known as mirage (Asadi et al. 2026), is especially concerning in medical and document visual question answering, where plausible but visually ungrounded responses may be mistaken for image-based evidence. We study pre-release mirage detection: given an image-question pair, the goal is to determine whether a VLM should answer or abstain before producing a response. We propose Text-Conditioned Layer-wise Internal Alignment (TC-LIA), a model-agnostic method that probes patch-token representations across the layers of a CLIP ViT-H/14 vision encoder. TC-LIA projects layer-wise image patch tokens into the final CLIP embedding space and measures their similarity to the question embedding, allowing the method to track whether question-relevant visual evidence emerges across vision layers. The resulting alignment trajectory is summarized using final image-text cosine similarity, late-layer top-k patch-text alignment, early-to-late gain, and layer-wise slope. These features are combined with pixel-statistic blank/noise detection, zero-shot domain routing, and structured VLM self-assessment in an ensemble. Across five VQA domains, three input conditions, and twelve VLM backbones, the best systems achieve approximately 94.6-94.7% three-class detection accuracy with mirage rates below 3%, while baseline mirage rates range from 21.7% to 66.6%.

</details>


### [46] [Physical Object Understanding with a Physically Controllable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00439)
*Rahul Venkatesh,Klemen Kotar,Lilian Naing Chen,Wanhee Lee,Gia Ancone,Seungwoo Kim,Luca Thomas Wheeler,Jared Watrous,Honglin Chen,Daniel Bear,Stefan Stojanov,Daniel LK Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的概率性世界模型，通过自回归序列建模从原始视频中学习场景的物理结构，能够推断视觉变量并从中提取对象及其运动规律，实现对3D对象的操作和物理关系分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型难以从部分观测中推断世界的分布状态，无法有效学习场景的物理结构和对象交互规律。

Method: 引入一类支持任意视觉变量条件概率估计的概率性世界模型，并利用自回归序列建模进行高效训练，通过多步未来状态生成与运动相关性分析来发现对象及子部件。

Result: 模型能生成多个合理的未来状态，成功提取对象和可动部件，实现3D操作，并计算对象间的物理关系，应用于如视觉版Jenga等任务。

Conclusion: 该方法为从视频中学习场景结构和物理规律提供了有效途径，展现出强大的推理与对象理解能力。

Abstract: A central challenge in visual intelligence is learning the physical structure of scenes from raw videos: how regions form objects and the laws that govern their interactions. Solving these tasks requires world models capable of inferring distributional states of the world from partial observations - capabilities that current architectures do not provide. We introduce a new class of probabilistic world models that support estimation of the probability of any visual variable, such as appearance and dynamics, conditioned on any other variables. Here, we identify that these models can be trained efficiently with autoregressive sequence modeling, yielding world models from which rich object understanding emerges. First, we demonstrate that our model captures the physical laws governing how objects move by generating multiple plausible future states of the world through sequential inference. Then, by analyzing motion correlations across these futures, we extract objects and articulated object subparts. Having discovered these objects, we show that our world model can manipulate them in 3D. Finally, we demonstrate how physical relationships between objects can be computed from the world model, enabling applications such as Visual Jenga.

</details>


### [47] [Real-Time Physics Simulation with Dynamic Mesh-Gaussian Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00444)
*Adrian Ramlal,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将动态3D重建集成到物理模拟中的挑战，提出了一种双表示框架，通过固定拓扑结构进行物理模拟，利用高斯点阵进行渲染，实现了比可变拓扑基线快4.65倍的速度。然而，评估显示转换方法在几何质量上退化了65-80%，结果不如原生固定拓扑方法。研究指出高质量重建与物理兼容拓扑是两个不同的目标，无法通过后处理调和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态3D重建方法（如DG-Mesh）生成的网格拓扑结构变化，不利于高效的物理碰撞检测，因此需要探索如何在保持重建精度的同时实现与物理模拟的集成。

Method: 提出一种双表示框架，使用固定拓扑网格用于物理模拟，高斯点阵用于渲染，并通过运行时顶点缓冲区更新实现高效性能；比较了两种拓扑转换策略：时间对应跟踪和模板投影，并与原生固定拓扑方法（MaGS）在DG-Mesh数据集上进行评估。

Result: 所提框架实现了4.65倍的运行速度提升，但两种转换策略导致几何质量下降65-80%，最终效果仍不如MaGS等原生固定拓扑方法。

Conclusion: 高质量几何重建与物理兼容的固定拓扑是本质冲突的目标，仅靠后处理的拓扑转换无法解决；未来应发展物理感知的重建方法，而本文框架可支持任何固定拓扑方法实现实时模拟。

Abstract: Integrating dynamic 3D reconstructions into physics simulation requires fixed mesh topology for efficient collision detection, but state-of-the-art methods like DG-Mesh produce varying topology optimized for geometric quality. We investigate whether topology conversion can enable physics integration while preserving reconstruction fidelity. We propose a dual-representation framework combining fixed-topology meshes for physics with Gaussian splatting for rendering, achieving 4.65$\times$ speedup over varying-topology baselines through runtime vertex buffer updates. We evaluate two conversion strategies, temporal correspondence tracking and template-based projection, against native fixed-topology methods (MaGS) on the DG-Mesh dataset. Our evaluation reveals that both conversion approaches incur 65-80% geometric degradation, producing results inferior to MaGS despite DG-Mesh's superior initial quality. This demonstrates that high-quality reconstruction and physics-compatible topology represent fundamentally distinct objectives that cannot be reconciled through post-processing. Our findings inform future development of physics-aware reconstruction methods and our framework enables real-time simulation with any fixed-topology approach.

</details>


### [48] [DarkVesselNet: Multi-Modal Remote Sensing and Trajectory Reasoning for Dark Vessel Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00445)
*Arun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DarkVesselNet是一个多模态遥感系统，融合SAR、光学影像和AIS数据用于探测暗船，具备完整的软件实现与初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高对未广播AIS信号的船舶（暗船）的检测能力，需结合卫星遥感与航行数据进行多模态分析。

Method: 结合Sentinel-1 SAR、Sentinel-2光学影像、地理空间基础模型主干、AIS轨迹推理、TGARD式间隙检测和Pi-DPM启发的异常检测头，构建端到端的融合架构。

Result: 实现了传感器堆栈、主干抽象、融合路径和异常检测头，并通过多项技术测试验证了系统各组件的有效性，如SAR去斑、光学波段比、传感器配准等。

Conclusion: DarkVesselNet提供了一个可复现、可扩展的暗船检测框架，支持公开部署与进一步研究。

Abstract: Dark vessel detection requires fusing what vessels report through AIS with what satellites observe through radar and optical sensors. DarkVesselNet is a multi-modal remote sensing stack that combines Sentinel-1 SAR, Sentinel-2 optical imagery, geospatial foundation model backbones, AIS trajectory reasoning, TGARD-style gap detection, and a Pi-DPM-inspired anomaly head. The repository exposes the system as a tested Python package and a public Hugging Face Space. The paper presents the sensor stack, backbone abstraction, fusion path, anomaly head, and current validation. The evidence currently available is software-grounded: tests for SAR speckle filtering, optical band ratios, Haversine distance, TGARD gap emission, sensor coregistration, backbone token shapes, and differentiable anomaly scoring.

</details>


### [49] [GeoSAM-3D: Geodesic Prompt Propagation for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Segmentation from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00447)
*Arun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSAM-3D提出了一种轻量化的开放词汇3D场景分割方法，通过单目视频和用户点击或命名对象生成在高斯场景中传播的3D掩码，利用基于图测地距离的热核传播策略减少跨不连通物体的泄漏，并保持曲面连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D分割方法通常依赖RGB-D视频、多视角图像或重建网格，成本较高；GeoSAM-3D旨在探索更轻量、易用的设置，仅需用户上传单目视频并简单交互即可完成3D掩码传播。

Method: 结合冻结的图像与视频基础模型、单目3D高斯溅射重建，以及在高斯中心上可微分的图测地传播核；核心设计是使用场景图上的热核距离而非欧氏最近邻进行提示传播。

Result: 实现了对高斯场景中对象的有效3D掩码传播，减少了跨断开对象的泄漏，在弯曲表面上保持了更好的连续性，并验证了实现正确性、传播质量、抗泄漏能力及交互延迟。

Conclusion: GeoSAM-3D为开放词汇3D分割提供了一个高效且低门槛的交互式框架，其基于图热核的传播机制优于传统欧氏邻近传播，适合实际应用中的轻量级需求。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene segmentation usually assumes RGB-D video, calibrated multi-view imagery, or a reconstructed mesh. GeoSAM-3D studies a lighter setting: a user uploads a short monocular video, clicks or names an object in one frame, and receives a propagated 3D mask over a Gaussian scene. The implementation combines frozen image and video foundation models with a monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstruction and a differentiable graph-geodesic propagation kernel over Gaussian centroids. The central design choice is to propagate prompts by heat-kernel distance on the reconstructed scene graph, rather than by Euclidean nearest neighbors in 3D. This preserves continuity around curved surfaces and reduces leakage across nearby but disconnected objects. This paper describes the repository state, the mathematical kernel implemented in geosam3d.propagate, the feature head trained from Segment Anything masks, and the validation already present in the codebase. The evaluation protocol separates implementation validation, graph propagation quality, leakage control, and interactive latency.

</details>


### [50] [Optimizing 3D Gaussian Splatting via Point Cloud Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00450)
*Adrian Ramlal,Yan Song Hu,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出并评估了多种点云上采样方法以改进3D高斯点阵（3DGS）的初始化，并引入一种深度引导的点提升方法，实验表明不同上采样策略适用于不同类型场景，提升了3DGS重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯点阵（3DGS）的性能高度依赖初始种子点的质量，现有初始化方法在复杂或纹理缺失区域表现不足，因此需要更优的点云上采样策略来提升几何一致性和重建精度。

Method: 提出了多种点云上采样方法，包括线性插值、三角插值、基于样条曲面重建、移动最小二乘曲面拟合和Voronoi点生成，并引入一种利用深度图保持与SfM重建几何一致性的深度引导点提升方法。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF360和Replica数据集上的实验表明：曲面重建方法在有机、细节丰富的场景中表现更优，简单插值方法更适合分段平滑几何场景，深度引导方法能在整个场景（尤其是无纹理区域）有效添加几何感知点，显著提升3DGS初始化质量。

Conclusion: 不同的点云上采样策略适用于不同类型的场景，深度引导的点提升方法有助于增强几何一致性，研究结果为根据场景特征和计算约束选择合适上采样方法提供了实用指导，推进了对3DGS初始化影响的理解。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a technique for creating and rendering 3D scenes, however its performance depends heavily on the quality of initial seed points. To improve 3DGS initialization, this study presents and evaluates several point cloud upsampling approaches: linear interpolation, triangular interpolation, spline-based surface reconstruction, moving least squares surface fitting, and Voronoi-based point generation. Additionally, this research introduces a depth-guided point lifting method that leverages depth maps to maintain geometric consistency with Structure-from-Motion (SfM) reconstructions. Through extensive experiments on the Mip-NeRF360 and Replica datasets, the proposed methods demonstrate improvements in reconstruction quality across diverse scene types. Results indicate that different upsampling strategies excel in different scenarios: surface reconstruction methods perform better with organic, detailed scenes, while simpler interpolation approaches are more suited for scenes dominated by piecewise-smooth geometries. In comparison, the depth-guided approach shows promise for adding geometry-aware points across the entire scene, importantly in texture-less regions. These findings, which provide preliminary practical guidelines for selecting appropriate upsampling methods based on scene characteristics and computational constraints, advances the understanding of how point cloud initialization affects 3DGS quality.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Static Gaussians: An Empirical Investigation of Architectural Paradigms for Dynamic 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00452)
*Adrian Ramlal,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基于3D高斯点阵的动态场景重建方法进行了系统分析，将其分为结构引导和高斯基元中心两类范式，并在D-NeRF基准上进行评估，揭示了重建质量与渲染速度之间的核心权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同动态3D高斯点阵方法之间的性能权衡，推动动态场景重建技术的发展。

Method: 将现有方法分为结构引导型和高斯中心型两类，并在D-NeRF数据集上对代表性方法进行实验比较。

Result: 结构引导方法在重建精度和模型紧凑性方面更优，而高斯中心方法渲染速度更快，可实现实时渲染，但存储开销大且质量波动明显。

Conclusion: 动态3DGS方法存在重建质量/模型大小与渲染速度之间的根本性权衡，研究为未来方法设计和应用选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a compelling approach for representing evolving environments, yet understanding trade-offs between methodologies remains crucial. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of dynamic 3DGS methods, categorizing them into two paradigms: structure-guided methods employing auxiliary representations (deformation fields, canonical spaces, grids) to model temporal changes, and gaussian-centric methods encoding dynamics directly into primitives via continuous functions or 4D representations. We evaluate representative methods from both paradigms on the D-NeRF benchmark. Our findings reveal that structure-guided methods achieve superior reconstruction fidelity and compact model sizes, while gaussian-centric approaches demonstrate significantly higher rendering speeds enabling real-time performance, though with greater quality variability and potentially substantial storage overhead. This analysis highlights a fundamental trade-off between reconstruction quality/compactness versus rendering speed, providing insights to guide future research and application development in dynamic scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [52] [An explainable hierarchical self attention-based approach for tremor detection in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00461)
*Timothy Odonga,Jeanne M. Powell,Mark Saad,Richa Tripathi,Christine D. Esper,Stewart A. Factor,Hyeokhyen Kwon,J. Lucas Mckay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的两阶段分层框架，用于在时间域内直接从3D运动学数据中学习震颤模式，实现跨身体部位的震颤检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统震颤诊断依赖专家评估，现有自动化方法依赖频域特征，本研究旨在减少对专家设计特征的依赖，探索数据驱动的时间域建模方法。

Method: 结合深度卷积和长短期记忆网络提取短时段运动学数据特征，再利用视觉Transformer建模长期时序动态以进行会话级分类。

Result: 在九个身体部位上F1分数为0.594-0.947（平均0.765），低于频域最优性能（0.909），但预处理需求更少；通过注意力权重和Grad-CAM实现了特征可视化与解释。

Conclusion: 验证了时间域数据驱动建模在多部位震颤检测中的可行性，提供了良好的可解释性，减少了对频域特征工程的依赖。

Abstract: Tremor is a common movement disorder associated with conditions like Parkinson's disease and Essential tremor, traditionally diagnosed through expert clinician assessment. Current automated detection methods rely on frequency-domain features informed by clinical expertise. In this work, we present an explainable, two-stage hierarchical framework for tremor detection in the time domain that learns tremor patterns directly from 3D kinematic marker time-series data across entire tremor-provoking trials. Our framework combined a deep convolutional and long short-term memory network to learn tremor representations from short, discrete, non-overlapping time segments of kinematic time series data from trials, which are then processed by a vision transformer that models their long-term temporal dynamics of time segment features for trial (session) level classification. Evaluated across nine body parts, the framework achieved F1-scores of 0.594 - 0.947 depending on body parts (average: 0.765), falling short of the frequency-domain state-of-the-art performance (0.909) while requiring minimal preprocessing. Attention weights and gradient-based class activation maps (Grad-CAM) identified time-domain features of tremor across body parts. This proof of concept demonstrated the feasibility of data-driven time-domain modeling for tremor detection across anatomically diverse body parts, while reducing reliance on expert-engineered spectral features and providing posthoc interpretability of temporal and anatomical patterns of tremor.

</details>


### [53] [MUSCLE-NET: Predicted-Multiscale-Aware Network for Pedestrian Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00471)
*Yu Liu,Ming Huang,Xiao Ren,Zhijie Liu,Youfu Li,He Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于行人轨迹预测的多尺度感知网络MUSCLE-Net，通过多模态特征提取和层次化预测机制提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在充分利用多源观测信息和处理未来运动的尺度依赖性方面存在不足，导致对多样化行人行为的鲁棒性受限。

Method: 设计了多尺度多模态特征提取（MMFE）模块和多尺度增强型层次预测（MEHP）模块，结合多尺度表示、模态感知重校准和方向性跨模态融合，并通过概率粗预测器、尺度对齐融合与渐进优化实现自适应预测。

Result: 在JAAD和PIE基准上的实验表明，MUSCLE-Net相比现有最先进方法具有竞争力的性能和稳定的增益。

Conclusion: MUSCLE-Net能有效整合多模态线索并自适应利用多尺度特征，提升了行人轨迹预测的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate pedestrian trajectory prediction is essential for safe navigation in autonomous driving and intelligent transportation systems. Despite substantial progress made by recent methods, most existing approaches are limited in fully exploiting diverse observations and often overlook the scale dependency of future motion, treating multiscale features uniformly regardless of underlying motion dynamics. This limits their robustness across diverse pedestrian behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose a Predicted-MUltiSCale-Aware Network (MUSCLE-NET) for Pedestrian Trajectory Forecasting that integrates complementary multimodal cues with scale-adaptive prediction mechanisms. The proposed framework is built upon a Multiscale Multimodal Feature Extraction (MMFE) module, which combines multiscale representation, modality-aware recalibration, and directional cross-modal fusion to construct semantically aligned representations from bounding boxes, velocities, and pose information. Building on these features, a Multiscale Enhanced Hierarchical Prediction (MEHP) module performs prediction-aware future-motion refinement via a probabilistic coarse predictor, scale-aligned fusion, and progressive refinement, adaptively selecting scale-relevant cues to mitigate spatial drift. Extensive experiments on the JAAD and PIE benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed MUSCLE-Net achieves competitive performance and consistent gains compared with state-of-the-art trajectory prediction methods.

</details>


### [54] [CodeCytos: AI-assisted spatial molecular imaging analysis via code-augmented agent action space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00472)
*Hung Q. Vo,Huy Q. Vo,Son T. Ly,Zhihao Wan,Anh-Vu Nguyen,Hong Zhao,Jianting Sheng,Stephen T. C. Wong,Hien V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeCytos是一个基于代码推理的智能体框架，旨在提升空间分子成像数据的自动化与定制化分析能力，支持无需复杂指令即可探索自定义细胞空间特征，并通过领域无关的少样本示例显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织图像分析工具依赖人工干预、缺乏代码驱动自动化、灵活性差，难以满足复杂且多样化研究需求。

Method: 提出CodeCytos框架，利用具备强编码能力的大语言模型实现动态编程式交互；在极简提示下测试其表现，并引入领域无关的少样本推理示例以提升效果。

Result: 在四个不同组织类型的专家标注数据集上验证了框架有效性，CodeCytos优于基线方法，尤其在无任务特定指导的情况下表现出色。

Conclusion: CodeCytos展示了代码动作智能体在空间分子成像中推动自定义特征探索和加速生物标志物发现的潜力。

Abstract: Conventional tissue image analysis software provides foundational capabilities for cellular analysis, including segmentation, basic morphological feature extraction, and spatial organization analysis. However, these tools often require manual intervention and are not well integrated with code-driven automation, limiting efficiency and scalability for complex spatial tissue studies. In addition, they offer limited flexibility for custom analyses, as they typically support only a fixed set of pre-implemented spatial cellular features. To address these limitations, we propose CodeCytos, a coding-based reasoning agent framework that enables dynamic, programmable interaction with spatial molecular imaging data to improve automation and customization. CodeCytos is designed to streamline the exploration of custom spatial cellular features and adapt to diverse research needs. We demonstrate its utility through case studies on four expert-curated datasets from distinct tissue types: frontal cortex, non-small-cell lung cancer, pancreas, and tonsil. We evaluate CodeCytos under a realistic minimal prompt setting, where bioscientists pose simple questions without task-specific instructions or contextual information about spatial cellular analysis, and benchmark multiple LLM backbones with strong coding capabilities. We further show that incorporating tailored, domain-agnostic few-shot in-context coding-reasoning examples (randomly sampled demonstrations outside the spatial analysis domain) can substantially improve performance without requiring costly, expert-crafted in-domain demonstrations. Overall, CodeCytos outperforms baseline approaches, highlighting the potential of code-action agents to assist with custom feature exploration in spatial molecular imaging and to accelerate biomarker discovery.

</details>


### [55] [3D Segment Anything Model with Visual Mamba for Diagnosing Placenta Accreta Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00489)
*Yuliang Zhang,Fang He,Lulu Peng,Tianyu Yan,Pingping Zhang,Ting Song,Lili Du,Dunjin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MRI的自动胎盘植入谱系（PAS）诊断方法，构建了首个包含精细分割与分类标注的PAS数据集，并提出了3DSAMba框架用于病灶分割，显著提升了诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于基层医院缺乏经验丰富的医生和资源，传统依赖专家分析剖宫产史和MRI数据的PAS诊断方式难以普及，亟需一种自动化、准确的诊断方法。

Method: 设计了一个3D Segment Anything Model，通过适配器机制引入医学领域信息；提出多级聚合Mamba（MLAM）和融合状态空间模型（FSSM）以聚合和融合多尺度特征；利用分割掩码与原始MRI进行逐元素相乘，实现病灶区域隔离。

Result: 在自建的MRI-based PAS数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了所提框架在PAS诊断中的有效性，显著提升了诊断性能。

Conclusion: 3DSAMba框架结合专用数据集可有效提升PAS的自动诊断准确性，具有临床应用潜力，且数据和代码已公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Placenta Accreta Spectrum (PAS) is a rare but highly dangerous obstetric disease. Early and accurate PAS diagnosis is critical for maternal health. Traditional PAS diagnosis relies on experienced doctors by analyzing the cesarean history and Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) data. However, district-level hospitals often lack the expertise and resources for accurate PAS diagnosis. To address these challenges, we establish the first MRI-based PAS dataset, which includes both fine-grained segmentation and classification annotations. Meanwhile, diagnosing PAS can be significantly enhanced by segmenting lesion areas from MRI images of the uterus. To achieve automatic PAS diagnosis, we propose 3DSAMba, a novel feature learning framework for effective lesion segmentation. More specifically, we first design a 3D Segment Anything Model (SAM) and incorporate medical domain information into the model through an efficient adapter mechanism. In addition, we introduce a Multi-Level Aggregation Mamba (MLAM) to aggregate feature maps across different levels and a Fusion State Space Model (FSSM) to fuse multi-scale features from both the encoder and decoder. Finally, we apply segmentation masks to the original MRI images through element-wise multiplication, effectively isolating lesion areas for more accurate PAS diagnosis. Extensive experiments validate that our framework significantly improves the PAS diagnostic performance. To facilitate further research in PAS diagnosis, we have released the dataset and source code at https://github.com/Drchip61/PASD.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [56] [DraDDP: A Multimodal Multi-Party Dialogue Discourse Parsing Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00012)
*Shannan Liu,Peifeng Li,Yaxin Fan,Qiaoming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于多说话人对话话语解析的英文多模态数据集DraDDP，基于美剧构建，包含495个对话片段、6,374条语句和9.1小时视频，并建立了全面的基准评测，验证了多模态信息在对话结构和关系识别中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话话语解析研究大多局限于文本模态或双人对话，难以应对真实场景中多说话人和多模态交互的复杂性，因此需要构建更贴近现实的多模态多说话人数据集以推动研究发展。

Method: 基于美国电视剧构建多模态多说话人对话话语解析数据集DraDDP，包含人工标注的依存结构和关系类型；建立多模态基准评测框架，分析不同模态对任务性能的影响。

Result: DraDDP数据集包含495个对话段、6,374条语句和9.1小时视频；实验表明引入多模态信息能显著提升对话结构和关系类型的识别效果；建立了首个公开可用的多模态多说话人话语解析基准。

Conclusion: 多模态信息对理解多说话人对话结构具有重要意义，DraDDP为未来多模态对话理解研究提供了重要资源，有望推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Multi-party dialogue discourse parsing aims to identify dependency structures and relation types between utterances in conversations. Previous studies are mostly limited to textual modality or two-party dialogue, failing to meet the multimodal and multi-party settings. In this paper, we construct the first publicly available English multimodal dataset DraDDP for multi-party dialogue discourse parsing, based on American TV dramas. DraDDP contains 495 dialogue segments with 6,374 utterances and 9.1 hours of parallel video content, covering rich multi-party interaction scenarios. Moreover, we establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating this task on DraDDP and conducting in-depth analysis on the impact of different modalities. Experimental results demonstrate the value of multimodal information in capturing dialogue structures and relation types. We will publicly release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and code to promote future research in multimodal dialogue understanding.

</details>


### [57] [Toward Robust In-Context Learning: Leveraging Out-of-distribution Proxies for Target Inaccessible Demonstration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00014)
*Hao Xu,Rite Bo,Fausto Giunchiglia,Yingji Li,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DOPA的演示搜索框架，通过引入OOD代理和基于马氏距离的多样性约束，提升大模型在分布外任务中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在目标域不可访问时难以评估未知分布，导致选择的示例质量下降，影响大模型在严重分布偏移下的推理能力。

Method: 设计了一个包含OOD代理的演示搜索框架DOPA，用以近似不可访问的目标域并指导检索；同时引入基于马氏距离的全局多样性约束，确保检索样本的多样性。

Result: 在多个大语言模型和任务上的实验表明，DOPA能有效提升模型在分布外场景下的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DOPA通过代理建模和多样性控制，在无法获取目标域的情况下显著提升了跨分布推理的示范选择质量。

Abstract: Although studies have demonstrated that Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform well on Out-of-Distribution (OOD) tasks, their advantage tends to diminish as the distribution shift becomes more severe. Consequently, researchers aim to retrieve distributionally similar and informative demonstrations from the available source domain to boost the inference capabilities of LLMs. However, in practical scenarios where the target domain is inaccessible, evaluating the unknown distribution is challenging, which indirectly impacts the quality of the selected demonstrations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{DOPA}, a demonstration search framework that incorporates an OOD proxy to approximate the inaccessible target domain and guide the retrieval process. Building on proxy-based evaluation, DOPA further introduces a Mahalanobis distance-based global diversity constraint to ensure sufficient diversity among the retrieved demonstrations. Experimental results on multiple LLMs and tasks demonstrate that DOPA effectively enhances robustness in OOD settings\footnote{https://github.com/bort64/ood\_code}.

</details>


### [58] [AEyeDE: An Attention-Based Attribution Framework for AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00016)
*Aria Nourbakhsh,Adelaide Danilov,Christoph Schommer,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力归因的AI生成文本检测方法AEyeDE，利用代理Transformer模型提取注意力归因矩阵，并通过轻量级卷积神经网络进行学习，在多种设置下表现出色且具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越来越接近人类水平的流畅度，传统的基于表面统计或似然信号的检测方法难以有效区分人类与AI生成文本，因此需要更可靠和可解释的检测手段。

Method: 使用具有白盒访问权限的代理Transformer模型提取人类和AI生成文本的注意力归因矩阵，训练轻量级卷积神经网络从这些归因图中学习表征以实现作者归属检测。

Result: 在编码器-解码器翻译任务中优于纯文本基线；在仅解码器设置中对特定生成器检测表现良好，跨数据集迁移和拼写扰动下仍具鲁棒性；注意力图显示人机生成文本在局部结构频率上有系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的归因图提供了一种互补且可解释的AI生成文本检测信号，有助于提升检测性能和模型可解释性。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text is becoming increasingly challenging as modern language models approach human-level fluency and can evade detectors that rely on surface statistics or likelihood-based signals. We propose \textsc{AEyeDE}, an attribution-driven approach to human-AI authorship detection that leverages model attention as a discriminative signal. Specifically, we extract attention-based attribution matrices for both human- and AI-generated text using a \emph{proxy} Transformer model with white-box access and train a lightweight Convolutional Neural Network to learn representations from these attribution maps. Across encoder-decoder translation settings, our method consistently outperforms a text-only baseline. In decoder-only settings, it performs strongly in generator-specific detection, remains competitive on standard benchmarks, and shows robustness under cross-dataset transfer and alternative-spelling perturbations. We further show that attention maps exhibit recurring local structures whose relative frequencies differ consistently between human- and AI-generated text across datasets and proxy models. These findings suggest that attention-based attribution maps provide a complementary and interpretable signal for AI-generated text detection. We will make the code publicly available to support future research.

</details>


### [59] [CSRP: Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Chinese Text Correction via Reinforcement Learning with Efficiency-Aware Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00020)
*Wei Tian,Yuhao Zhou,Man Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段框架CSRP，用于提升基于大语言模型的中文语法纠错性能，通过持续预训练、链式思维微调和组相对策略优化，显著提高了精确率并缓解了过纠错问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型缺乏对细微语法差异的语言先验知识，且传统的监督微调方法在精确性指标上优化不足，容易导致系统性过纠错。

Method: 提出CSRP框架：第一阶段在590万平衡样本上进行持续预训练以融入领域知识；第二阶段采用链式思维监督微调，增强错误推理的可解释性；第三阶段使用带效率感知奖励的组相对策略优化，显式惩罚不必要的修改。

Result: 在NACGEC基准上达到50.99的F0.5和57.17的精确率，显著优于此前最优结果；在CSCD拼写纠错任务上F1达59.61，超过GPT-4 5.20个百分点；消融实验显示强化学习对齐阶段比SFT基线相对提升8%，且与大规模预训练效果正交。

Conclusion: 显式优化编辑效率对于高质量语法纠错至关重要，CSRP通过分阶段融合领域知识与精细化训练策略，有效提升了中文语法纠错的性能并抑制了过纠错现象。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (CGEC) systems face two critical challenges: general-purpose models lack specialized linguistic priors for subtle grammatical distinctions, and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Maximum Likelihood Estimation fails to optimize for precision-focused metrics, leading to systematic over-correction. We propose CSRP, a three-stage framework that progressively builds correction capability through Continual Pre-training (CPT) on 5.9M balanced samples to internalize domain knowledge, Chain-of-Thought SFT with explicit error reasoning for diagnostic transparency, and Group Relative Policy Optimization with a novel Efficiency-Aware Reward that explicitly penalizes unnecessary edits. On the NACGEC benchmark, CSRP achieves state-of-the-art performance with 50.99 $F_{0.5}$ and 57.17 precision, substantially outperforming previous best results while effectively mitigating the over-correction bias inherent in MLE-trained models. Our method also advances CSCD spelling correction to 59.61 F1, surpassing GPT-4 by 5.20 points. Comprehensive ablation studies demonstrate that the RL alignment stage contributes a 8\% relative gain over the SFT baseline, and that this gain is orthogonal to the contribution of large-scale CPT, validating that explicit optimization for edit efficiency is essential for high-quality grammatical error correction. Our code is available at https://github.com/TW-NLP/ChineseErrorCorrector.

</details>


### [60] [SENSE: Semantic Embedding Navigation with Soft-gated Evaluation for Retrieval-based Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00021)
*Shaowen Chen,Zhicheng Liao,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SENSE通过基于目标模型隐藏状态的语义检索和软门控评估模块，提升检索式推测解码的鲁棒性和效率，在多领域实现最高3.26倍的推理加速，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式推测解码（RSD）方法受限于对表层词汇的刚性依赖，导致检索与验证过程对表面变化敏感而脆弱，影响推理效率与稳定性。

Method: 提出SENSE框架：利用目标模型的隐藏状态进行语义锚定检索，并设计软门控评估模块判断语义等价性而非表面匹配；构建统一框架以分解现有方法为原子组件，实现细粒度比较。

Result: 在LLaMA和Qwen系列模型上实验表明，SENSE达到最高4.09的平均接受长度和3.26倍推理速度提升，显著优于多个基线方法，且保持生成质量。

Conclusion: SENSE通过语义对齐和软门控验证机制，有效克服了传统RSD方法对词汇表层形式的依赖，为高效、鲁棒的推测解码提供了新范式。

Abstract: Speculative Decoding (SD) accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) inference by employing a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens, which are verified in parallel by the target model, without compromising generation quality. While Retrieval-based Speculative Decoding (RSD) is favored for its plug-and-play versatility, its potential is impeded by rigid lexical dependencies, rendering both retrieval and verification brittle to surface-level variations. To address this, we propose SENSE (Semantic Embedding Navigation with Soft-gated Evaluation). By anchoring retrieval on the hidden states of the target model, SENSE establishes robust semantic alignment, which empowers the Soft-gated Evaluation module to validate semantic equivalence rather than surface forms. To ensure rigorous benchmarking, we deconstruct existing methods into atomic primitives within a unified framework, facilitating granular, component-level comparison. Extensive experiments across diverse domains demonstrate that SENSE outperforms multiple baselines on the LLaMA and Qwen families, attaining up to 4.09 mean acceptance length and 3.26x speedup, while preserving generation quality. Our code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [61] [lmfaoooo at SemEval-2026 Task 1: Humor Is an Audience. Preference Modeling for Constrained Humor Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00022)
*Alexey Tikhonov,Alexey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“生成-筛选”策略的幽默生成系统，在SemEval-2026 Task-1（MWAHAHA）中取得优异成绩，利用多步提示、模型集成和偏好建模，结合人类成对比较数据提升生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于幽默具有受众依赖性强、标注噪声大、评价主观等特点，自动生成“有趣”的内容仍然极具挑战性，传统依赖绝对评分的监督方法效果有限。

Method: 采用“生成多样候选 -> 基于偏好模型筛选”的两阶段策略：通过多步提示、模型集成和多样性解码生成候选池，并利用从人类成对比较中学习的偏好模型选择最佳输出。

Result: 在三个偏好数据集上，所提模型均优于基线并表现出更强的跨域迁移能力；在MWAHAHA任务中，该系统在英语和中文子任务中排名第一，西班牙语子任务中排名第二。

Conclusion: 基于人类比较的偏好建模能更有效地指导幽默生成，所提出的可解释流程和发布的数据集（含2.5K人工对比标注、候选池和排序结果）有助于推动后续研究。

Abstract: Humor generation remains difficult not only because producing fluent, novel jokes is hard, but because "funny" is audience-dependent and supervision is noisy -- preferences vary with audience, context, and culture, and annotator agreement is often low. In this paper, we describe our system for the SemEval-2026 Task-1 (MWAHAHA), which focuses on humor generation under explicit constraints. The task evaluates submitted systems via human preference judgments in 1-on-1 arena-style comparisons.
  We adopt a "generate-many -> select-best" strategy. First, we generate a diverse pool of candidates per instance using multi-step prompting, model ensembling, and diversity-oriented decoding. Second, we select outputs using a preference model that approximates a "reader" by learning from human comparisons rather than absolute funniness scores. To support this approach, we release 2.5K human pairwise judgments collected through the Humor Arena prototype. We further propose an interpretable pipeline that converts labeled comparisons into a preference model. Across three preference datasets, our models consistently outperform baselines and show stronger cross-domain transfer. Finally, we apply the learned preference model to rank candidates for the MWAHAHA setting and release intermediate artifacts (candidate pools and rankings) to facilitate follow-up work.
  Our system ranked 1st in the English and Chinese subtasks of MWAHAHA and 2nd in the Spanish subtask.

</details>


### [62] [TrustLDM: Benchmarking Trustworthiness in Language Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00023)
*Yichuan Mo,Yukun Jiang,Yanbo Shi,Mingjie Li,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对语言扩散模型（LDMs）的可信性基准TrustLDM，评估其在安全、隐私和公平性方面的表现，并揭示了在恶意上下文下模型对齐行为的退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言扩散模型的发展，其灵活的解码策略带来了新的可信性挑战，亟需系统评估其在不同架构下的安全性、隐私性和公平性。

Method: 构建了一个名为TrustLDM的综合性可信性基准，并提出了TrustLDM-Auto自动化评估框架，利用LDM的解码灵活性来识别脆弱配置。

Result: 实验表明，尽管LDM在仅使用用户提示时表现良好，但在附加恶意上下文时对齐行为明显下降；上下文长度、解码顺序和生成长度均影响评估结果。

Conclusion: 所有被评估的模型和维度均暴露出显著的可信性弱点，该研究有助于社区构建更可信的LDMs。

Abstract: The rapid development of Language Diffusion Models (LDMs) challenges the dominant position of auto-regressive competitors in language processing. However, their flexible, any-order decoding strategies not only enable fast decoding speed but also potentially bring new trustworthiness challenges. To better understand the risks behind their pipelines, we introduce a comprehensive trustworthiness benchmark tailored to LDMs (TrustLDM), evaluating safety, privacy, and fairness across different LDM architectures with multiple categories of static post contexts. Our empirical results show that although LDMs generally exhibit strong trustworthiness with only the user prompts, their alignment behavior degrades noticeably when the malicious post contexts are attached to the masked responses. We further observe that longer contexts do not necessarily induce stronger effects, and both decoding order and generation length affect the evaluation outcomes. Finally, we propose TrustLDM-Auto, an automatic evaluation framework that leverages LDM decoding flexibility to systematically identify vulnerable configurations, revealing substantial trustworthiness weaknesses across all evaluated models and dimensions. Our work may potentially help the community build more trustworthy LDMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ML/TrustLDM.

</details>


### [63] [ART: Attention Run-time Termination for Efficient Large Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00024)
*Chen Qiu,Guozhong Li,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attention Run-time Termination (ART)的轻量级运行时机制，用于提升大语言模型在长上下文解码中的生成吞吐量，通过在内核执行过程中动态终止对KV缓存的访问，在保持准确率的同时显著减少内存带宽消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受限于庞大的KV缓存带来的内存带宽压力，现有方法多基于键进行剪枝，但忽略了值的实际影响，且引入值会带来过高开销。因此需要一种兼顾注意力输出联合依赖性且低开销的方法。

Method: 提出ART机制，在解码过程中动态跟踪注意力输出的累积值，并在后续KV块贡献可忽略时提前终止对其的访问，该机制在运行时实现且与现有的基于键的KV缓存管理方法正交，可无缝集成。

Result: 在LongBench基准测试中，ART相比当前最先进的基线方法在大批量生成时实现了20%更高的吞吐量，同时保持了相当的准确性。

Conclusion: ART是一种高效、轻量且兼容性强的长上下文解码优化方法，能够显著提升大语言模型的生成效率，为KV缓存管理提供了新的运行时优化维度。

Abstract: Long-context decoding in Large Language Models (LLMs) is severely constrained by the memory bandwidth required to fetch the extensive Key-Value (KV) cache. Most existing KV management methods rely on key-only pruning before decoding, despite the evidence that attention outputs depend jointly on keys and values, as incorporating values in their methods incurs prohibitive additional overhead. In this paper, we propose Attention Run-time Termination (ART), a lightweight run-time mechanism that tracks accumulated attention outputs during kernel execution and terminates subsequent KV block accesses once further contributions become negligible. This design makes ART orthogonal to existing key-based KV cache management methods, enabling seamless integration with them. Experiments on LongBench benchmarks show that ART achieves 20% higher generation throughput in large batch size than state-of-the-art baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [64] [Cognitive-Linguistic Indicators of Depression in Online Communities: Analysed by DistilBERT and Holographic Reduced Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00026)
*Brian Van Steen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究结合认知语言学特征与DistilBERT嵌入，构建混合模型以提升在线文本中抑郁症的自动检测效果，结果表明该方法显著优于传统TF-IDF基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于贝克的抑郁认知理论，探索认知扭曲语言特征（如第一人称代词密度、绝对化用语和负面情绪）在抑郁症检测中的作用，并检验其与先进嵌入方法融合的有效性。

Method: 从Reddit帖子中提取认知语言特征，构建两种分类流程：基于TF-IDF与朴素贝叶斯的基线模型，以及将DistilBERT句子嵌入与HRR向量（编码认知特征）拼接后输入逻辑回归的混合模型。

Result: 混合DistilBERT-HRR模型的宏F1得分为0.94，显著高于TF-IDF基线的0.80；五折交叉验证中F1从0.83提升至0.92，AUC从0.958提升至0.981。

Conclusion: 融合认知理论驱动的语言特征与预训练Transformer嵌入可显著提高抑郁症文本检测性能，验证了认知启发特征在心理健康自然语言处理任务中的价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether combining cognitively grounded linguistic features with transformer-based embeddings improves automated detection of depression in online text. Using Beck's Cognitive Theory of Depression, the study extracts cognitive distortions as measurable features, including first-person pronoun density, absolutist words, and negative emotion in Reddit posts from depression-related and control communities. Using a subset of the Kaggle Reddit Suicide and Depression Detection dataset, two classification pipelines are compared, a TF-IDF embedding with Naive Bayes as a baseline, and a hybrid model that concatenates DistilBERT sentence embeddings with Holographic Reduced Representation (HRR) vectors encoding the cognitive-linguistic features, followed by Logistic Regression. The hybrid DistilBERT HRR model achieves a macro F1 score of 0.94 versus 0.80 for the TD-IDF baseline, with 5-fold cross validation F1 improving from 0.83 to 0.92, and AUC from 0.958 to 0.981.

</details>


### [65] [A Multi-Domain Red Teaming Framework for Safety, Robustness, and Fairness Evaluation of Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00027)
*Andrei Marian Feier,Veysel Kocaman,Yigit Gul,Ahmet Korkmaz,Alexander Thomas,Aleksei Zakharov,Jay Gil,Mehmet Butgul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个多领域红队测试框架，评估了11个大型语言模型在690个临床场景中的表现，发现平均准确率掩盖了临床上重要的风险，尤其是在安全关键场景中的个别完全失败。研究表明，最坏情况下的表现和性能差异比平均精度更能反映临床可靠性，且结合自动化与临床医生监督的混合评估方法对可信安全评估至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型基准未能捕捉到临床实践中常见的对抗性或伦理复杂条件下的模型行为，因此需要更全面、更具临床相关性的评估框架来识别潜在风险。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖九个领域、150多个子类别的多领域红队测试框架，包含690个基于临床的对抗性场景，使用七维度评分标准进行评估，并结合LLM辅助打分与人工验证的混合评估方式。

Result: 11个LLM的平均得分介于0.791至0.984之间，高性能模型在个别安全关键场景中仍出现完全失败；X-BAI、GPT-5和Claude Opus 4.1表现最佳（>0.97），但不同领域表现差异显著；涉及公平性的任务在人口统计学变化下错误率增加10-20%；人工评审发现了自动化评估未捕获的临床相关错误。

Conclusion: 仅依赖平均准确率无法充分反映LLM在临床应用中的真实风险，性能方差和最坏情况表现是更具有临床意义的可靠性指标，必须采用人机协同的混合评估方法以确保安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across healthcare, yet existing benchmarks fail to capture model behavior under adversarial or ethically complex conditions common in clinical practice. We developed a multi-domain red teaming framework evaluating eleven contemporary LLMs across 690 clinically grounded scenarios spanning nine domains and over 150 subcategories. Scenarios incorporated adversarial transformations, and responses were assessed using a seven-dimension rubric with LLM-assisted scoring and human-in-the-loop validation. Results revealed substantial performance variance, with mean scores ranging from 0.791 to 0.984. Critically, several high-performing systems produced complete failures in individual safety-critical scenarios, demonstrating that aggregate accuracy masks clinically meaningful risk. The highest-performing systems (X-BAI, GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.1) achieved scores above 0.97 with low variance, while performance varied significantly across domains. Equity-related tasks showed 10-20% error amplification with demographic modifications, and human reviewers identified clinically relevant failures missed by automated evaluation. Our findings demonstrate that performance variance and worst-case failures provide more clinically meaningful reliability indicators than mean accuracy alone, and that hybrid evaluation approaches combining automation with clinician oversight are essential for credible safety assessment.

</details>


### [66] [TCAR-Gen: Temporal Graph Retrieval with Evidence Fusion for Knowledge-Grounded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00029)
*Sidra Nasir,Muhammad Noman Zahid,Rizwan Ahmed Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TCAR-Gen框架，结合查询条件图神经网络、时间证据融合和链式树推理，提升基于历史犯罪叙事的复杂问题回答能力，在Victorian Crime Diaries基准上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成系统在处理涉及时间推理和多证据融合的复杂问题时存在不足，尤其在历史犯罪案例问答中难以有效整合多源信息。

Method: 设计TCAR-Gen框架，引入查询条件图神经网络进行语义感知检索，利用时间证据融合机制整合多个证据，并采用链式树推理实现多跳与反事实问题求解。

Result: 在Victorian Crime Diaries数据集上，TCAR-Gen取得0.3738 Recall@5，优于Vanilla RAG、Temporal RAG、GraphRAG-C和GraphRAG-T；消融实验显示上下文图、时间惩罚机制和查询条件是关键组件；跨模型测试表明其在小模型上保持良好检索覆盖但生成质量随模型缩小而下降。

Conclusion: 显式的时间建模和多分支证据融合对基于知识库的复杂推理问答至关重要，TCAR-Gen为时间敏感型任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems struggle with temporal reasoning and evidence fusion when answering complex questions over historical criminal case narratives. Existing approaches either retrieve independently of query semantics or fail to integrate multiple evidence sources coherently. We propose Temporal Context Augmented Retrieval Generation (TCAR-Gen), a framework that combines query-conditioned graph neural networks, temporal evidence fusion, and chain-of-trees reasoning to ground answer generation in retrieved evidence. On the Victorian Crime Diaries benchmark, TCAR-Gen achieves 0.3738 Recall@5, outperforming Vanilla RAG, Temporal RAG, GraphRAG-C, and GraphRAG-T across seven query types including multi-hop reasoning and counterfactual questions. Ablation studies reveal that the context graph, temporal penalty mechanism, and query conditioning are critical components. Cross-model evaluation across five language model (GPT-OSS 20B to TinyLlama 1.1B) demonstrates that TCAR-Gen maintains robust retrieval coverage at smaller model scales, though generation quality degrades substantially with reduced model capacity. Our work shows that explicit temporal modelling and multi-branch evidence fusion are essential for faithful, reasoning-intensive question answering over knowledge-grounded corpora.

</details>


### [67] [LLMs for Cardiovascular Risk Prediction from Structured Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00031)
*Jeba Maliha,Md Rafiul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合结构化临床数据与大语言模型生成的自然语言叙述的混合框架，用于冠状动脉疾病预测，在保证数据隐私的同时实现了较高的预测一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持冠心病的早期诊断和风险评估，需要开发能够利用自然语言处理能力的可靠预测系统。

Method: 将结构化变量转换为可解释的特征表示和由大语言模型生成的合成临床叙述，并通过反向提取计算与原始记录的一致性得分；比较传统机器学习模型与基于大语言模型（GPT和Gemini）在零样本和少样本提示下的分类性能。

Result: 结构化数据与生成叙述之间的平均保真度达到94.61%；随机森林准确率最高，但大语言模型在直接处理自然语言描述方面具有保护敏感数值数据隐私的优势。

Conclusion: 结合结构化临床数据与大语言模型生成的叙述可为混合型临床预测系统提供新方向，尤其在保护患者隐私方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Coronary artery disease (CAD) remains one of the leading causes of death globally, highlighting the need for reliable predictive systems to support early diagnosis and risk assessment. While traditional machine learning models perform well on structured clinical data, large language models (LLMs) present new possibilities to interpret medical information expressed in natural language. In this work, we develop a hybrid framework that bridges structured clinical data and natural-language representations for CAD prediction. Using a publicly available dataset of 1,190 patient records with 11 clinical attributes, structured variables are converted into interpretable feature representations and synthetic clinical narratives using LLMs. A validation pipeline performs reverse extraction of clinical variables and computes a consistency score with the original records, achieving an average fidelity of 94.61%. We then evaluate four conventional machine learning models and compare their performance with LLM-based classification under zero-shot and few-shot prompting settings. We use two LLMs here, GPT and Gemini. Experimental results show that Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy. Despite this advantage, LLM-based classification remains beneficial in real-world clinical settings. This is because LLMs operate directly on natural language patient descriptions, meaning that sensitive numerical patient data such as exact lab values, blood pressure readings, and diagnostic codes are kept private. Findings suggest that combining structured clinical data with LLM-generated narratives can enable new directions for hybrid clinical prediction systems.

</details>


### [68] [Graph-Augmented Retrieval for Cross-Entity Financial Sentiment Analysis: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00062)
*Rajan Bastakoti,Sagar Bhetwal,Nirajan Acharya,Gaurav Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于两跳图结构的Graph-RAG架构，用于跨实体金融情感分析，相较于传统向量检索，在实体召回和答案相关性上显著提升，且未牺牲语义准确性，为金融领域的关系型信息检索提供了有效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于向量的RAG系统难以捕捉金融分析中复杂的多实体结构化关系，因此需要一种能利用知识图谱进行关系推理的新方法以提升跨实体情感分析的效果。

Method: 构建一个包含59个股票实体、基于255篇科技股新闻的情感加权知识图谱，采用两跳图遍历结合强度过滤的INFLUENCES边增强密集检索，并与纯向量检索基线进行对比；通过语义相似度、实体召回率、RAGAS指标、延迟和消融实验评估性能。

Result: Graph-RAG在实体召回率上提升6.4%（p < 0.001），对复杂多实体查询的答案相关性提升11.7%（关系类问题达16.1%），语义相似度无损，平均延迟增加22.6%但延迟方差降低80%；消融实验发现图遍历强度阈值tau=0.5最优，呈现倒U型关系。

Conclusion: Graph-RAG在不损害答案质量的前提下显著提升了对多实体关系的检索能力，揭示了图增强检索中精度与覆盖的权衡，为金融领域的RAG系统设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become foundational for grounding large language models in domain-specific corpora, yet conventional vector-based RAG systems are fundamentally limited in their ability to capture the structured, multi-entity relationships that underpin financial market analysis. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative study of a novel two-hop Graph-RAG architecture versus a standard vector-only baseline for cross-entity financial sentiment analysis. Our system constructs a sentiment-weighted knowledge graph of 59 equity entities from 255 news articles covering 10 major technology stocks, then augments dense retrieval with intensity-filtered graph traversal over INFLUENCES edges to surface relational evidence inaccessible to vector search alone.
  We evaluate both architectures on 100 grounded queries (30 Direct, 70 Relational) using semantic similarity, entity recall, RAGAS metrics, latency benchmarks, and ablation studies. Graph-RAG achieves a statistically significant improvement in entity recall (+6.4%, p < 0.001, Wilcoxon signed-rank) and delivers substantially more relevant answers for complex multi-entity queries (+11.7% Answer Relevancy), with gains concentrating in relational question types (+16.1%). Critically, these improvements come at no measurable cost to answer quality (delta = +0.001 semantic similarity, Cohen's d = 0.078), with a modest 22.6% increase in mean latency offset by an 80% reduction in latency variance.
  An ablation study on the graph traversal intensity threshold reveals an inverted-U relationship with answer quality, identifying tau = 0.5 as optimal over the production default of tau = 0.7. These findings characterize a precision-for-coverage trade-off inherent to graph-augmented retrieval and provide actionable architectural guidance for practitioners building RAG systems for multi-entity financial analysis.

</details>


### [69] [DLLM-JEPA: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00091)
*Sangdae Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLLM-JEPA将JEPA与掩码扩散语言模型结合，消除对显式多视图数据和双前向传播的依赖，降低训练计算量并提升多项任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-JEPA继承了因果注意力机制的高成本问题，需要显式多视图数据和两次梯度传播，限制了效率与应用范围。

Method: 提出DLLM-JEPA，利用掩码扩散语言模型的双向注意力特性，通过不同掩码率生成语义不同的输入视图，并实现单次梯度前向传播。

Result: 相比LLM-JEPA，训练FLOPs减少33%；在GSM8K、Spider、NL-RX-SYNTH、Django等多个任务上性能显著提升，最高提升达+18.7个百分点；同时改善遗忘问题并保持预训练知识。

Conclusion: DLLM-JEPA通过结合扩散模型有效解决了JEPA在语言建模中的效率与效果瓶颈，展现出一致的性能增益和更好的表示保持能力，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have reshaped self-supervised representation learning in vision. The recent LLM-JEPA ported JEPA to autoregressive language models but inherited two steep costs from the causal-attention substrate: it demands explicit multi-view data (e.g., text-code pairs), and it requires two gradient-carrying forward passes per step. We introduce DLLM-JEPA, which pairs JEPA with masked-diffusion language models to eliminate both costs at once. The bidirectional attention of diffusion models yields two semantically distinct views of the same input via different masking rates -- no explicit pairs needed -- and supports a single gradient-carrying forward pass, cutting training FLOPs by 33% relative to LLM-JEPA. DLLM-JEPA improves over diffusion-only fine-tuning in every (task, architecture) combination we evaluate: up to +18.7 pp on LLaDA-8B GSM8K and +11.4 pp on Dream-7B GSM8K, with consistent positive gains on Spider, NL-RX-SYNTH, and Django. Beyond accuracy, DLLM-JEPA exhibits a dual-win property: on LLaDA-8B with the Wide-t configuration, it simultaneously raises GSM8K accuracy (67.1 vs. 65.2, +1.8 pp), drives held-out Wikitext loss below the pre-trained base, and preserves MMLU accuracy at base level across three fine-tuning seeds -- whereas an L2-to-base parameter anchor matches baseline accuracy with no task gain. Layer-wise probing reveals the mechanism: a geometric-functional drift dissociation in which the fine-tuned backbone moves further from the pre-trained weights than the baseline yet forgets less on held-out Wikitext, with the amplification concentrated in middle transformer layers. The pattern appears on Dream-7B as well, indicating the phenomenon is not specific to a single backbone.

</details>


### [70] [Agreement Metrics for LLM-as-Judge Evaluation: What to Report and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00093)
*Delip Rao,Chris Callison-Burch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在使用人类标注验证大语言模型（LLM）评判时，常用一致性统计指标的选择问题，指出在二元判断中多个指标实际上是冗余的，Cohen's κ 提供了额外信息，并分析了弃权处理方式对结果的影响，最后提出了一个报告检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前LLM-as-judge研究中指标选择不透明、冗余的问题，特别是在二元判断和弃权处理上的混淆。

Method: 通过调查24篇近期论文，分析不同评价指标在二元数据下的数学等价性，探讨弃权处理方式的影响，并扩展到多评判者场景。

Result: 发现Pearson相关、Spearman秩相关、Kendall τ_b、phi系数和Matthews相关系数在非退化二元数据下均等价于单一数值；Cohen's κ 提供额外信息，反映评判者正类率偏移；三种常见的弃权处理方式实质上回答不同问题且破坏二元等价性；在多评判者场景中，Fleiss' κ 和 Krippendorff's α 在有限样本修正下恢复等价性。

Conclusion: 应避免报告多个等价指标造成佐证假象，需明确说明判断尺度、弃权与平局处理方式、覆盖率、混淆矩阵及聚合层级，建议使用提出的报告检查清单以提高透明度和可比性。

Abstract: Validating an LLM judge against human annotations usually means reporting several agreement statistics: accuracy, precision, recall, $F_1$, Cohen's $κ$, and one or more rank correlations. A survey of 24 recent LLM-as-judge papers finds metric choice entangled with the judgment scale, tie handling, invalid outputs, and abstention handling, and those choices rarely stated. For binary criteria -- the common case in rubric-based evaluation, where each criterion is graded MET or UNMET -- most of the reported numbers are redundant: Pearson's $r$, Spearman's $ρ$, Kendall's $τ_b$, the phi coefficient $φ$, and the Matthews Correlation Coefficient all reduce to a single number on non-degenerate binary data, so reporting several of them only creates an illusion of corroborating evidence. Cohen's $κ$ is the one agreement coefficient that adds information: it shares $φ$'s numerator but normalizes differently, and the gap between them measures how far the judge's positive-label rate has drifted from the human's. We then trace what changes when a judge may abstain with a CANNOT_ASSESS verdict: the three common ways of handling abstentions are not interchangeable preprocessing choices but answer different questions, and they break the binary equivalences. The same equivalences reappear, up to a negligible finite-sample correction, for multi-judge ensembles scored with Fleiss' $κ$ or Krippendorff's $α$. We close with a reporting checklist that names the judgment scale, the abstention and tie handling mode, coverage, the confusion matrix, and the aggregation level alongside any scalar agreement coefficient.

</details>


### [71] [Enhancing BiGRU with a KAN Block for Legal Document Classification and Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00116)
*Ahmed Faizul Haque Dhrubo,Souvik Pramanik,Most. Aysha Siddika Sumona,Shahnewaz Siddique,Mohammad Ashrafuzzaman Khan,Mohammad Abdul Qayum,Mohsin Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KAN的BiGRU模型，用于低资源多语言法律文档的分类与摘要生成，在处理领域语言、多语言使用、长距离依赖和类别不平衡方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低资源多语言环境下法律文档分类与摘要面临的领域术语、语言多样性、上下文长距离依赖及类别不平衡等问题。

Method: 采用包含Bengali、English和转写Bengali的Bangladesh法律文书数据集，分类任务使用结合KAN模块的BiGRU模型，摘要任务采用带KAN头部的注意力机制GRU模型。

Result: 分类准确率为67.96%，F1得分为0.65；摘要的ROUGE-1、ROUGE-2和ROUGE-L F1得分分别为0.38、0.23和0.31；消融实验显示KAN使分类准确率从57.34%提升至67.96%。

Conclusion: 所提KAN增强模型在低资源多语言法律文本处理中优于多种基线方法，验证了KAN在提升模型性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: This study introduces a novel architecture of KAN-based BiGRU model for the task of classification and summarization of legal documents in a low-resource multilingual setup. In order to tackle problems associated with domain language, the usage of different languages, long dependencies within context, and class imbalance, we employ the dataset composed of legal documents from Bangladesh and taken from Manupatra, which include Bengali, English, and transliterated Bengali languages. Our classification task involves BiGRU model, along with Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) module, while the summarization part utilizes attention-based GRU, combined with a KAN model head. Classification model yields 67.96% of accuracy and 0.65 F1 score; while ROUGE-1, ROUGE-2, and ROUGE-L measures for summarization yield 0.38, 0.23, and 0.31 F1 scores, correspondingly. Ablation study shows that the use of KAN increases classification accuracy from 57.34% to 67.96%. Moreover, our proposed technique is compared to several baselines, including classical ML algorithms and pretrained language models.

</details>


### [72] [RealityTest: How People Probe AI Identity and Whether Models Disclose It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00168)
*Anna Gausen,Sarenne Wallbridge,Bessie O'Dell,Christopher Summerfield,Hannah Rose Kirk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RealityTest是一个大规模、多模态、多语言的基准测试，用于评估AI系统在真实对话场景中是否主动披露其身份，研究发现人类提出的关于AI身份的问题具有高度多样性，且模型的披露行为受提问方式和上下文影响较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地应用于对话场景，用户可能难以分辨对话对象是人类还是AI，存在安全隐患；然而现有评估方法多局限于英语和机器生成问题，缺乏真实人类数据支持，因此需要更全面、贴近现实的评估框架。

Method: 构建名为RealityTest的多模态多语言基准，基于来自49个国家约750名参与者的真实数据，收集了3,152个文本和语音形式的身份探查问题，并对17个文本模型和6个语音模型进行测试，分析不同模型在多种条件下的身份披露表现。

Result: 只有31%的人会在模糊情境下直接询问对方是否为AI，人类提出的问题比机器生成的更加多样；不同模型在披露行为上表现出显著差异，但一条抑制指令即可使披露率降至30%以下；提问方式和对话上下文对披露结果的影响超过模型本身。

Conclusion: 基于狭窄或合成数据集的安全评估可能无法准确反映模型在实际部署中的行为，使用多样化、基于真实人类交互的数据对于构建可靠的安全评测至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly deployed in conversational settings where users may be uncertain whether they are speaking with a human or an AI. Despite mounting regulatory attention to this known safety risk, existing evaluations of AI disclosure are typically English-only, based on machine-generated questions, and restricted to text. We present RealityTest to comprehensively test whether AI systems disclose their identity when asked. The benchmark is the first large-scale multimodal and multilingual evaluation, grounded in human data on how people actually encounter and question AI identity in the real-world. Alongside the benchmark, we release the underlying dataset of 3,152 identity-probing queries collected from ~750 participants across 49 countries and five languages, in text and speech scenarios. We find that only 31% of people ask about identity directly in ambiguous scenarios, and that the questions people ask are far more diverse than machine-generated queries. We test 17 text and 6 speech models, and find substantial variation in disclosure behaviour. However, a single suppression instruction reduces disclosure rates to below 30%, even in the best-performing models. Validating our investment in diverse, human-grounded evaluation data, we find that how the question is phrased and the context of the conversation matter more for disclosure than which model is being tested. Safety evaluations built on narrow or synthetic query sets risk mischaracterising how models behave in realistic deployment settings.

</details>


### [73] [BOUTEF: A Multilingual Corpus for FakeNews in North Africa -- Language as a Weapon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00193)
*Kamel Smaili,Yassine Toughrai,Amina Laggoun,David Langlois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BOUTEF，一个用于研究阿尔及利亚和突尼斯虚假新闻传播的大规模多语言语料库，并通过定量与定性分析揭示了虚假新闻在主题、语言策略、情感模式和社会参与方面的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对北非等多语言、资源匮乏环境中虚假新闻快速传播的问题，缺乏合适的多语言研究语料库，因此构建BOUTEF以支持相关研究。

Method: 构建包含虚假信息、真实信息、用户评论及核实辟谣内容的多语言语料库，涵盖MSA、阿尔及利亚和突尼斯方言、阿拉伯字母转写、法语、英语及语码混合现象；结合定量统计与定性分析方法，分析主题分布、语言修辞策略、情感模式和社交互动动态。

Result: 发现主题类别与消息真实性之间存在显著关联，用户参与度与虚假内容可见性高度相关；虚假新闻多使用情绪化叙述、耸动框架和混合语言策略以增强传播力，而辟谣内容则更注重事实核查；阿尔及利亚与突尼斯在虚假新闻传播上既有共性也有因社会政治背景导致的差异。

Conclusion: 非正式语言实践在错误信息的传播与接受中起关键作用，BOUTEF语料库为低资源语言环境下的虚假新闻检测与信息紊乱研究提供了重要支持。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news on social media has become a major challenge, particularly in multilingual and under-resourced contexts such as North Africa. In this paper, we introduce BOUTEF, a large-scale multilingual corpus designed to study the propagation, characteristics, and impact of fake news in Algeria and Tunisia. The corpus integrates three complementary components: fake narratives, genuine narratives, and associated user-generated comments, along with verified debunking information. It covers a wide range of languages and linguistic varieties, including MSA, Algerian and Tunisian dialects, Arabizi, French, English, and code-switched language. Building on this resource, we conduct a comprehensive empirical analysis combining quantitative and qualitative approaches. We examine thematic distributions, linguistic and rhetorical strategies, sentiment patterns, and social engagement dynamics. Statistical analyses reveal significant associations between thematic categories and message veracity, as well as strong correlations between user engagement and the visibility of fake content. Our findings show that fake news relies heavily on emotionally charged narratives, sensational framing, and hybrid linguistic practices that enhance virality and audience engagement. In contrast, debunking content adopts a more factual and verification-oriented style.
